Let's consider using "stochastic dual ascent" to optimize the b coefficients. The objective max b. We could formulate a primal-dual game: find a in simplex to minimize max k of a^T T_k a. The dual might involve distributions over k: For any probability w over lags, min_a? If we choose w, the minimax lower bound: for any w, max_k b_k ≥ ∑ w_k b_k = a^T (∑w_k T_k) a. We can lower bound objective by max over w of min? Actually to design a small max, need for all w? The maximum over k of b_k = sup_{w on simplex} ∑w_k b_k. Could use alternating: choose w distribution of active lags, optimize a for ∑w b_k, update w at max. But optimizing weighted quadratic nonconvex (T_w not PSD). However maybe T_w PSD if w is a probability over all convolution lags? Let's examine: ∑ w_k b_k = ∑_{i,j} a_i a_j W_{i,j}, where W_{ij}=w_{i+j} (for i+j<2n-1). W is a Hankel matrix. Is W PSD for arbitrary w? Not necessarily. But if w is chosen as positive definite sequence? The matrix with entries w_{i+j} is Toeplitz-Hankel. Not guaranteed.

Maybe use "short-time" autocorrelation: Weighted sum over contiguous lags corresponds to nonnegative? If w_k = 1/(L) for k in interval, W is a band-diagonal with sliding sum, maybe PSD? For convolution b, ∑_{k=m}^{m+L-1} b_k = ∑_{i,j: i+j in interval} a_i a_j. Matrix is a band of ones (i+j between m andm+L-1), a Toeplitz anti-diagonal band. Is this PSD? It is a persymmetric band with ones on bands. Eigenvalues? For L large, maybe PSD? For L=1, single anti-diagonal matrix not PSD. So no.

High-level plan:
1. Define efficient objective functions using FFT for normalized sum=1: `convolution_self(a)`, `max_conv(a)`, `evaluate_internal(a)` returns 2n*maxconv/(sum^2) (but sum1). For speed, use `np.convolve`? FFT.
2. Functions to build candidate sequences:
   a. Previous height_sequence_1 if available (scale to max 1000).
   b. Low-dimensional parametrized families:
      - Piecewise polynomial/spline: use B-spline basis of order m over [0,1] with nonnegative coefficients? Generate vector of length n: f(x)=exp(v)? Or polynomial. Need easy to evaluate for many n and optimize parameters.
      - Trigonometric polynomial: f(x)=exp? Actually f must nonnegative. Use softmax of features: f_i=exp(∑ c_k φ_k(x_i))/sum. Continuous shape with nonnegative. We can optimize features? Too many.
      - A small set of basis functions (e.g., 6 B-splines) with log weights, search weights using global local methods. This can represent smooth asymmetric shapes. The objective as function of weights is smooth (with positive f). Use gradient perhaps.
   But maybe simpler: use random smooth vectors (Gaussian process) with global optimization over a few Fourier coefficients.
3. Full optimizer on best reduced init:
   - Softmax L-BFGS-B with increasing beta; multi-start from several reduces and previous.
   - FISTA/Adam-like projected gradient with exact line search, maybe with momentum; use LSE and top-k.
   - Multi-scale: run on downsampled length (e.g., 1024/2048) to find shape, upsample to 8192 via interpolation, polish. This can escape local minima and find continuous optimum. Good idea!
      - Downsampling: For length n0 small (e.g., 512,1024), optimize full vector with FISTA/L-BFGS; then upsample to target length n=8192 by interpolation (e.g., eval scipy? Or simple linear/cubic). This can find coarse shape, then polish. Because continuous optimum has smooth features, low-res search can catch global basin. The previous program used fixed length 8192 and local; multi-scale can be different.
4. Because objective scales invariant, downsample/up sampling: If optimize at n small, objective asymptotic; higher n better. Use coarse n=512,1024,2048,4096,8192. For each, start from previous downsampled or random. Run time-limited FISTA/soft L-BFGS at each. Track best objective at current n and final. Actually final objective desired at any length; we can output length with best objective (maybe 8192 or different). But evaluate_sequence uses np.convolve direct, so larger length cost; keep target maybe 8192 or 16384. 5. Multi-scale algorithm details:

`softmax_lse_fun` should handle `v` as 1D ndarray; if beta negative? no.
```
def softmax_lse_fun(v,beta,S):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    z=v-v.max(); e=np.exp(z); p=e/e.sum()
    b=fft_conv_self(p)
    maxb=b.max(); shifted=beta*(b-maxb); shifted-=shifted.max(); se=np.exp(shifted).sum()
    obj=(maxb + math.log(se)/beta)*(S*S)
    w=se? w = np.exp(shifted)/se
    grad_p=conv_gradient(p,w)
    grad_v=grad_p - p*float(np.dot(grad_p,p))
    # multiply by S^2
    return obj, grad_v*(S*S)
```

Implement continuous family generator:
```
def make_beta_family(n, alpha, beta, S, eps=0.5):
    i=np.arange(n)
    x=(i+eps)/n
    # to avoid overflow for beta negative and x near1: use log
    logv = -alpha*np.log(x) + beta*np.log1p(-x)
    logv -= np.max(logv)  # relative
    v=np.exp(logv)
    v *= S / v.sum()
    return v
```
If alpha=0, logx term zero (but log(0)? x>0). If beta=0, log1p(-x) zero but log1p(-0.999) finite negative. v.sum positive. `actual_obj(a)`:
```
if len(a)==0: return inf
a=np.asarray(a,float)
s=a.sum()
if s < 1e-15: return inf
c=fft_conv_self(a)
return float(2.0*len(a)*c.max()/(s*s))
```
Assume a nonnegative.

`fft_conv_self(a)`: 
```
n=len(a); size=2*n-1; fsize=1<<(size-1).bit_length()
A=fft(a.astype(np.float64), fsize)
c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:size]
# fix tiny negatives? no
return c
```
For n=1, size1, bit_length? (0).bit_length? handle: `if size <= 1: fsize=1`.

`project_simplex(v,S)`:
Use known stable:
```
v=np.asarray(v,float)
if S<=0: return zeros
n=len(v)
u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
totsum=np.cumsum(u)
rho = np.arange(1,n+1)
cond = u - (totsum - S)/rho > 0
if not any: return np.full(n,S/n)
theta = (totsum[rho-1] - S)/rho # need rho index
return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Be careful with rho. Use `idx=np.where(cond)[0][0]`, rho=idx+1, theta=(totsum[idx]-S)/rho.

`conv_gradient(a,w)`:
```
n=len(a); L=1 << (len(w)+n-2).bit_length()? Previous used `len(w) + n - 1 - 1` but let's derive: convolution length len(w)+n-1. next pow2 >= that. Use `L=1 << (len(w)+n-1-1).bit_length()`; if len(w)+n-1=1? ensure.
ar=a[::-1]
conv=np.real(ifft(fft(w,L)*fft(ar,L)))
return 2.0 * conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check slice length n: end 2n-1 exclusive gives indices n-1..2n-2 length n. Good.
If w length 2n-1. If n=1, slice [0:1]. Good.

`lse_weights(b,beta)`:
```
cm=b.max(); shifted=beta*(b-cm); shifted-=shifted.max(); e=np.exp(shifted); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: uniform; else e/s
```
`topk_gradient(a,b,k,power)`: sort indices of b descending. We can use heapsort? O(k log n) with `np.argpartition`:
```
if k>=len(b): idx=np.arange(len(b))
else: idx=np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]  # not sorted, okay
vals=b[idx]; w=vals**power; w/=w.sum(); w_full=zeros; w_full[idx]=w; return conv_gradient
```
For topk, weights not normalized? Sum 1 okay. `exact_subgradient`: active tolerance; weight uniform.

FISTA:
```
def fista_search(a0,budget,rng,beta_start=10.0,beta_max=5000.0,mode='hybrid',step_scale=1.0,noise=True):
    n=len(a0); S=math.sqrt(2*n)
    a=np.clip(a0,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-15: a=np.ones(n)*S/n
    else: a=a*(S/s)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0; start=time; it=0; best=a.copy(); best_obj=actual_obj(a); last=0; stuck=0
    min_time=0? while time-budget:
       c=fft_conv_self(y); maxc=c.max()
       obj=2*n*maxc/(S*S) # since y sum S
       if obj<best_obj-1e-12: best...
       beta=beta_start*(1+0.002*it); beta=min(beta_max, max(beta,beta_start))
       # weights
       if mode=='lse': w=lse_weights
       elif mode=='topk': k...
       elif mode=='hybrid': if stuck>800: exact tol else lse
       grad=...
       gnorm=...
       if gnorm<1e-14: break
       step=step_scale/max(beta,1.0)
       # optionally BB: step = min(step, S/(gnorm*(1+it*1e-5)))? Use previous.
       x=project_simplex(y-step*grad,S)
       t_new=...
       y=x+((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a); y=max(y,0); a=x; t=t_new
       if it%5000==0: t=1
       # noise if stuck>2000
    return best,best_obj
```

Add structured noise: random vector generated in time, low-pass via FFT? Use top-level `_smooth_noise(rng,n,scale)`.
```
def smooth_noise(rng,n,scale):
    x=rng.normal(0,1,n)
    F=fft(x)
    cut=max(1, n//100)
    F[cut:-cut]*=0.1
    x=np.real(ifft(F))[:n]
    norm=np.linalg.norm(x)
    if norm>1e-15: x=x/norm*scale
    return x
```

Now, exact code details for `resample_to_length`:
```
def resample_to_length(a,L):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1)
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return np.ones(L)/L
    if n==L: return a.copy()
    old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,L)
    v=np.interp(new,old,a)
    return v
```
For downsample, linear interpolation not average; may smooth. For multi-scale, okay. If we need preserve sum, normalize later.

```
    n=len(a0); S=sqrt(2*n)
    a=np.clip(a0...); normalize S
    best_a=a; best_obj=actual_obj(a)
    v0 = np.log(np.maximum(a/S, 1e-300)); v0=np.clip(...); v0-=v0.max()
    bounds=[(-30,30)]*n
    elapsed...
    for beta, this_maxiter in schedule:
       if elapsed>budget: break
       v=v0.copy(); v-=v.max()
       res=minimize(...)
       if res.x is None: res.x=v0
       v0=np.nan_to_num(res.x, nan=0.0, posinf=30.0, neginf=-30.0)

       p=softmax_vector(v0)  # need top-level
       a_new=S*p
       obj=actual_obj(a_new)
       if obj<best_obj-1e-12: update
    # final resample? no
    return best_a, best_obj
```

```
def smooth_noise(rng,n,scale):
    if n<=1: return np.zeros(n)
    x=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    F=fft(x)
    cut=max(1,n//100)
    if cut*2 < n:
       F[cut:n-cut]*=0.1
    x=np.real(ifft(F))[:n]
    nn=np.linalg.norm(x)
    if nn>1e-15: x=x*(scale/nn)
    else: x=rng.normal(0,scale,n)
    return x
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The program combines:
  1. A low-dimensional continuous “power-law” family
        a_i ~ x_i^(-alpha) * (1-x_i)^beta,
     which has proven near-optimal finite-n behavior for this problem.
  2. Projected FISTA with log-sum-exp / exact subgradient phases.
  3. Softmax-reparameterized L-BFGS-B polishing of a smoothed autocorrelation.

The returned list is normalized to sum 1, which is scale-invariant for the
objective and avoids clipping in evaluate_sequence().
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize

N_FINAL = 8192


def fft_conv_self(a):
    """Full self-convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if size <= 1:
        fsize = 1
    else:
        fsize = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:size]
    return c


def actual_obj(a):
    """Exact objective value (scale-invariant)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(a) == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    b = fft_conv_self(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * float(np.max(b)) / (s * s))


def conv_gradient(a, w):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w[k] * b[k] with b = convolve(a,a).

    For full convolution coefficients,
        db_k / da_j = 2 a_{k-j}  (when valid).
    """
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    length = len(w) + n - 1
    if length <= 1:
        L = 1
    else:
        L = 1 << (length - 1).bit_length()
    a_rev = a[::-1]
    conv = np.real(ifft(fft(w, L) * fft(a_rev, L)))
    return 2.0 * conv[n - 1: 2 * n - 1]


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Project v onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return v.copy()
    if S <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    tc = (cs - S) / idx
    valid = tc <= u
    if not np.any(valid):
        return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
    rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    theta = (cs[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def lse_weights(b, beta):
    """Weights for log-sum-exp approximation of max(b)."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    cm = float(np.max(b))
    shifted = beta * (b - cm)
    shifted -= shifted.max()
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    tot = float(e.sum())
    if tot < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(len(b), dtype=np.float64) / len(b)
    return e / tot


def exact_subgradient(a, b, tol_frac=0.0):
    """Subgradient from lags within tolerance of the maximum."""
    n = len(a)
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if mx <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if tol_frac > 0.0:
        active = np.where(b >= mx * (1.0 - tol_frac))[0]
    else:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=int)
    if len(active) == 0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=int)
    w = np.zeros(len(b), dtype=np.float64)
    w[active] = 1.0 / len(active)
    return conv_gradient(a, w)


def topk_gradient(a, b, k, power=1.2):
    """Gradient using only the top-k convolution coefficients."""
    n = len(a)
    k = max(1, min(k, len(b)))
    if k >= len(b):
        idx = np.arange(len(b))
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]
    vals = np.maximum(b[idx], 1e-300)
    w = np.power(vals, power)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws < 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / k
    else:
        w = w / ws
    w_full = np.zeros(len(b), dtype=np.float64)
    w_full[idx] = w
    return conv_gradient(a, w_full)


def smooth_noise(rng, n, scale):
    """Low-pass smooth random vector."""
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    F = fft(x)
    cut = max(1, n // 100)
    if 2 * cut < n:
        F[cut:n - cut] *= 0.1
    x = np.real(ifft(F))[:n]
    nn = float(np.linalg.norm(x))
    if nn < 1e-15:
        x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        nn = float(np.linalg.norm(x))
    if nn < 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return x * (scale / nn)


def fista_search(a0, budget, rng, beta_start=12.0, beta_max=4000.0,
                 mode="hybrid", step_scale=0.9):
    """
    Projected FISTA on the simplex.
    Uses LSE gradients for smoothness and exact/top-k subgradients when stuck.
    """
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a0)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = np.clip(a0, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (S / n)
    else:
        a = a * (S / s)

    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    it = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = actual_obj(a)
    last_improve = 0

    while True:
        if time.monotonic() - start >= budget:
            break
        it += 1

        b = fft_conv_self(y)
        cur = actual_obj(y)
        if cur < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = cur
            best_a = y.copy()
            last_improve = it
        stuck = it - last_improve

        base_beta = beta_start * (1.0 + 0.0018 * it)
        if stuck > 2500 and (it % 500 == 0 or rng.random() < 0.2):
            beta = max(beta_start, base_beta / (1.0 + stuck / 3000.0))
        else:
            beta = base_beta
        beta = min(beta_max, max(beta_start, beta))
        step = step_scale / max(1.0, beta)

        if mode == "topk":
            k = max(2, min(96, n // 16))
            if stuck > 800:
                k = min(k, max(2, 4 + stuck // 400))
            grad = topk_gradient(y, b, k, power=1.2)
        elif mode == "exact":
            tol = 0.0 if stuck < 100 else min(0.03, 0.002 + 0.00004 * stuck)
            grad = exact_subgradient(y, b, tol_frac=tol)
        else:  # hybrid
            if stuck < 700:
                w = lse_weights(b, beta)
                grad = conv_gradient(y, w)
            elif stuck < 2500 or (it % 7 != 0 and rng.random() > 0.35):
                w = lse_weights(b, max(beta, 50.0))
                grad = conv_gradient(y, w)
            else:
                tol = min(0.025, 0.003 + 0.00003 * max(0, stuck - 2500))
                grad = exact_subgradient(y, b, tol_frac=tol)

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            break

        # A mild Barzily-Borwein-like clamp avoids excessive single steps.
        if gnorm > 1e-15:
            step = min(3.0 * step, max(1e-6, 0.5 * S / gnorm))

        x = project_simplex(y - step * grad, S)
        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - a)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        a = x
        t = t_new

        if it % 5000 == 0:
            t = 1.0

        if stuck > 2500:
            noise_scale = (0.004 * S / math.sqrt(n)) * math.exp(-stuck / 8000.0)
            if noise_scale > 1e-7 and (it % 50 == 0 or rng.random() < 0.1):
                y = project_simplex(y + smooth_noise(rng, n, noise_scale), S)
                a = y.copy()
                t = 1.0
                last_improve = it

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    bs = float(best_a.sum())
    if bs > 0:
        best_a = best_a * (S / bs)
    return best_a, best_obj


def softmax_vector(v):
    """Stable softmax, sum = 1."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=30.0, neginf=-30.0)
    v = np.clip(v, -50.0, 50.0)
    z = v - float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(len(v), dtype=np.float64) / len(v)
    return e / s


def softmax_lse_fun(v, beta, S):
    """
    L-BFGS-B function: minimize S^2 * LSE_beta(conv(softmax(v), softmax(v))).
    Returns (objective, gradient).
    """
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=30.0, neginf=-30.0)
    v = np.clip(v, -50.0, 50.0)

    p = softmax_vector(v)
    b = fft_conv_self(p)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros_like(v)

    cm = float(np.max(b))
    shifted = beta * (b - cm)
    shifted -= shifted.max()
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se < 1e-300:
        weights = lse_weights(b, 1.0)
        se = 1.0
        cm = float(np.max(b))
    else:
        weights = e / se

    obj = (cm + math.log(max(se, 1e-300)) / beta) * (S * S)
    grad_p = conv_gradient(p, weights)
    dot = float(np.dot(grad_p, p))
    grad_v = (grad_p - p * dot) * (S * S)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_v)):
        grad_v = np.zeros_like(v)
    return obj, grad_v


def lbfgs_softmax_search(a0, budget, rng, schedule=None):
    """
    L-BFGS-B polishing in log-softmax variables.
    The softmax exposes the simplex exactly and often polishes FISTA results.
    """
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a0)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = np.clip(a0, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (S / n)
    else:
        a = a * (S / s)

    if schedule is None:
        # (beta, maxiter)
        schedule = (
            (6.0, 140),
            (20.0, 140),
            (80.0, 120),
            (350.0, 100),
            (1500.0, 80),
        )

    # Scale schedule to budget, but keep a sane minimum.
    factor = min(1.0, max(0.25, float(budget) / 360.0))

    v0 = np.log(np.maximum(a / S, 1e-300))
    v0 = np.clip(v0, -30.0, 30.0)
    v0 -= float(np.max(v0))

    bounds = [(-30.0, 30.0)] * n
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = actual_obj(a)

    start = time.monotonic()
    for beta, base_maxiter in schedule:
        if time.monotonic() - start >= budget:
            break
        maxiter = max(10, int(base_maxiter * factor))

        v = v0.copy()
        try:
            res = scipy_minimize(
                softmax_lse_fun,
                v,
                args=(beta, S),
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxls": 8,
                    "ftol": 1e-13,
                    "gtol": 1e-9,
                    "iprint": -1,
                },
            )
            new_v = res.x if res.x is not None else v
        except Exception:
            new_v = v

        new_v = np.asarray(new_v, dtype=np.float64)
        new_v = np.nan_to_num(new_v, nan=0.0, posinf=30.0, neginf=-30.0)
        new_v = np.clip(new_v, -30.0, 30.0)
        new_v -= float(np.max(new_v))
        v0 = new_v

        p = softmax_vector(new_v)
        cand = S * p
        obj = actual_obj(cand)
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj
            best_a = cand.copy()
            print("    L-BFGS beta=%7.2f obj=%.10f" % (beta, obj), flush=True)

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    bs = float(best_a.sum())
    if bs > 0:
        best_a = best_a * (S / bs)
    return best_a, best_obj


def make_beta_family(n, alpha, beta, S=1.0):
    """
    Non-negative power-law family:
        x_i = (i + 0.5) / n
        a_i ~ x_i^(-alpha) * (1-x_i)^(beta)
    Broadly captures the useful asymmetric endpoint-singular shapes.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    alpha = float(alpha)
    beta = float(beta)

    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (i + 0.5) / float(n)

    logv = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if alpha != 0.0:
        logv = logv - alpha * np.log(x)
    if beta != 0.0:
        logv = logv + beta * np.log1p(-x)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logv)):
        logv = np.nan_to_num(logv, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    logv -= float(np.max(logv))
    v = np.exp(logv)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    return v * (float(S) / s)


def beta_param_obj(params, n):
    """Objective for Nelder-Mead over alpha/beta."""
    if params is None or len(params) != 2:
        return 1e10
    alpha = float(params[0])
    beta = float(params[1])

    if not (math.isfinite(alpha) and math.isfinite(beta)):
        return 1e10
    if alpha < -0.02 or alpha > 0.502 or beta < -0.52 or beta > 4.0:
        return 1e10

    alpha = min(0.5, max(0.0, alpha))
    beta = min(4.0, max(-0.5, beta))

    a = make_beta_family(int(n), alpha, beta, S=1.0)
    val = actual_obj(a)
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return 1e10
    return float(val)


def resample_to_length(a, L):
    """Linear resample to length L (used for multistage/coarse starts)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, L)
    if a.size == L:
        return a.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
    return np.interp(new, old, a)


def upsample_vec(a, L):
    return resample_to_length(a, L)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    """
    Searches for a non-negative sequence of length 8192 minimizing the
    evaluation objective. It starts from the strong beta-family constructions,
    then polishes with softmax L-BFGS-B and FISTA. Always returns a valid list.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + (budget - 35.0)

    N = N_FINAL
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * N)

    best_a = None
    best_obj = float("inf")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1) Use previous construction if available.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    prev = None
    try:
        prev = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    if prev is not None and prev.size > 0:
        prev = np.nan_to_num(prev, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        prev = np.clip(prev, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ps = float(prev.sum())
        if ps > 1e-12:
            pN = resample_to_length(prev, N)
            sN = float(pN.sum())
            if sN > 1e-12:
                pN = pN * (S / sN)
            obj = actual_obj(pN)
            if math.isfinite(obj):
                best_obj = obj
                best_a = pN.copy()
                print("Previous construction (resampled): obj=%.10f" % obj, flush=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2) Low-dimensional power-law family.
    #    This family is the main global idea and typically jumps far below
    #    smooth local basins.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    best_alpha = 0.5
    best_beta = -0.25
    best_param_obj = float("inf")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0:
        n_scan = 2048

        alphas = np.array(
            [0.0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20, 0.25, 0.30, 0.35, 0.40, 0.45, 0.50],
            dtype=np.float64
        )
        betas = np.array(
            [
                -0.45, -0.42, -0.39, -0.36, -0.33, -0.30, -0.27, -0.24,
                -0.21, -0.18, -0.15, -0.12, -0.09, -0.06, -0.03, 0.00,
                0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.60, 1.00, 1.60,
            ],
            dtype=np.float64
        )

        print("Beta-family grid n=%d ..." % n_scan, flush=True)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 70.0:
                break
            for beta in betas:
                val = beta_param_obj((alpha, beta), n_scan)
                if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_param_obj:
                    best_param_obj = val
                    best_alpha = float(alpha)
                    best_beta = float(beta)
        print(
            "Beta grid best: obj=%.10f alpha=%.6f beta=%.6f time=%.1fs"
            % (best_param_obj, best_alpha, best_beta, time.monotonic() - t0),
            flush=True
        )

        # Local refinement on the scan length.
        scan_starts = [
            [best_alpha, best_beta],
            [0.50, -0.25],
            [0.50, -0.20],
            [0.50, -0.30],
            [0.45, -0.20],
            [0.40, -0.10],
        ]
        seen_scan = set()
        for x0 in scan_starts:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 80.0:
                break
            key = (round(float(x0[0]), 3), round(float(x0[1]), 3))
            if key in seen_scan:
                continue
            seen_scan.add(key)
            try:
                res = scipy_minimize(
                    beta_param_obj,
                    np.array(x0, dtype=np.float64),
                    args=(n_scan,),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 180,
                        "xatol": 1e-6,
                        "fatol": 1e-9,
                        "adaptive": True,
                    },
                )
                if res.fun is not None and math.isfinite(res.fun) and res.fun < best_param_obj:
                    best_param_obj = float(res.fun)
                    best_alpha = float(np.clip(res.x[0], 0.0, 0.5))
                    best_beta = float(np.clip(res.x[1], -0.5, 4.0))
                    print(
                        "Scan Nelder-Mead: obj=%.10f alpha=%.6f beta=%.6f"
                        % (best_param_obj, best_alpha, best_beta),
                        flush=True
                    )
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Generate final-length candidates from the current best parameters.
        final_starts = [
            [best_alpha, best_beta],
            [0.50, -0.25],
            [0.50, -0.20],
            [0.50, -0.30],
            [0.45, -0.18],
            [0.40, -0.10],
        ]
        seen_final = set()
        for x0 in final_starts:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 40.0:
                break
            al = float(np.clip(x0[0], 0.0, 0.5))
            be = float(np.clip(x0[1], -0.5, 4.0))
            key = (round(al, 3), round(be, 3))
            if key in seen_final:
                continue
            seen_final.add(key)

            a = make_beta_family(N, al, be, S)
            obj = actual_obj(a)
            if math.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
                best_obj = obj
                best_a = a.copy()
                print(
                    "Beta final candidate: obj=%.10f alpha=%.6f beta=%.6f"
                    % (obj, al, be),
                    flush=True
                )

        # Direct final-length refinement.
        final_nm_starts = [
            [best_alpha, best_beta],
            [0.50, -0.25],
            [0.50, -0.22],
            [0.48, -0.20],
        ]
        seen_nm = set()
        for x0 in final_nm_starts:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 50.0:
                break
            key = (round(float(x0[0]), 3), round(float(x0[1]), 3))
            if key in seen_nm:
                continue
            seen_nm.add(key)
            try:
                res = scipy_minimize(
                    beta_param_obj,
                    np.array(x0, dtype=np.float64),
                    args=(N,),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 140,
                        "xatol": 1e-6,
                        "fatol": 1e-9,
                        "adaptive": True,
                    },
                )
                if res.fun is not None and math.isfinite(res.fun):
                    if res.fun < best_param_obj:
                        best_param_obj = float(res.fun)
                    al = float(np.clip(res.x[0], 0.0, 0.5))
                    be = float(np.clip(res.x[1], -0.5, 4.0))
                    a = make_beta_family(N, al, be, S)
                    obj = actual_obj(a)
                    if math.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
                        best_obj = obj
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        print(
                            "Final Nelder-Mead: obj=%.10f alpha=%.6f beta=%.6f"
                            % (obj, al, be),
                            flush=True
                        )
            except Exception:
                pass

    # If no candidate has been found, guarantee a simple family.
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = make_beta_family(N, 0.50, -0.25, S)
        best_obj = actual_obj(best_a)
        print("Fallback beta candidate: obj=%.10f" % best_obj, flush=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3) Softmax L-BFGS-B polishing.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 80.0:
        lb_budget = min(900.0, max(40.0, remaining * 0.32))
        print("Starting L-BFGS-B polishing (budget %.0fs) ..." % lb_budget, flush=True)
        try:
            la, lobj = lbfgs_softmax_search(best_a, lb_budget, rng)
            if math.isfinite(lobj) and lobj < best_obj:
                best_obj = lobj
                best_a = la.copy()
                print("After L-BFGS-B: obj=%.10f" % best_obj, flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4) FISTA polishing.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 60.0:
        fista_budget = min(1400.0, max(40.0, remaining * 0.55))
        print("Starting FISTA polishing (budget %.0fs) ..." % fista_budget, flush=True)
        try:
            fa, fobj = fista_search(best_a, fista_budget, rng,
                                    beta_start=12.0, beta_max=4000.0,
                                    mode="hybrid", step_scale=0.9)
            if math.isfinite(fobj) and fobj < best_obj:
                best_obj = fobj
                best_a = fa.copy()
                print("After FISTA: obj=%.10f" % best_obj, flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5) Optional coarse-to-fine restart from the current best/previous.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 180.0 and best_a is not None:
        coarse_len = 1024
        try:
            a_c = resample_to_length(best_a, coarse_len)
            a_c, _ = fista_search(a_c, min(90.0, remaining * 0.35), rng,
                                  beta_start=10.0, beta_max=2500.0,
                                  mode="hybrid", step_scale=0.8)
            a_up = upsample_vec(a_c, N)
            su = float(a_up.sum())
            if su > 1e-12:
                a_up = a_up * (S / su)
            obj = actual_obj(a_up)
            if math.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
                best_obj = obj
                best_a = a_up.copy()
                print("Coarse-to-fine improved: obj=%.10f" % best_obj, flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # If previous sequence was better than the final N-resampled candidate,
    # we still prefer the best found at length N. The evaluator accepts any
    # length, but length 8192 is safe for direct np.convolve time.

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final normalization and return.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = make_beta_family(N, 0.50, -0.25, S)

    final = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    final = np.nan_to_num(final, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        final = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
        s = 1.0
    else:
        # Sum 1 keeps values far below clipping and preserves the objective.
        final = final / s

    final_list = [float(x) for x in final]

    # One final sanity check. If evaluate_sequence exists, verify.
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if math.isfinite(val):
            print("Verified evaluate_sequence objective: %.10f" % float(val), flush=True)
        else:
            print("WARNING: evaluate_sequence rejected final; using safe fallback.", flush=True)
            fallback = make_beta_family(N, 0.50, -0.25, 1.0)
            final_list = [float(x) for x in fallback]
    except Exception:
        pass

    print("Returned length: %d" % len(final_list), flush=True)
    return final_list
```
